# D4 TDD log

Each row is one `it should` case run alone (`-t` filter), so counts read "1 passed, N skipped". Complete cumulative suite runs are recorded in `structural.md` with full outputs in `suite-runs/`.

Every `it should` case from D4's breakdown, section 4, run red and then green, one at a time (kickoff prompt; breakdown, section 10). Each row is one run: the task, the case, the phase, the exact command, the exit code with Vitest's test summary, and an excerpt of the failure for red runs. A red row is valid only when it fails because the planned behavior is absent or incorrect; a red run that passes or fails for another reason is fixed and rerun, never skipped. Playwright reds and greens are logged by hand, because `tdd.sh` runs Vitest only.

| T4.14 | (task) | refactor | Explicit tie-break: `byAttention` now orders by band, comparable pressure descending, losing before winning, Last Activity descending, then Trade identity, so the order no longer depends on the Open list's page order (`lastActivity` is added to `ReviewTradeItem`); repeating the reversal mutation (reverse the derived Trades before sorting) leaves all 24 cases of `src/coordinators/daily-review/daily-review.test.ts` green. `open` split into `assemble` (one pass) and a retry loop. `pressureOf`, `attentionOf`, and the comparator live in `attention.ts` (pure, no Market Data import). Test names rephrased to drop apostrophes: "DEF Review-date Mark", "Trade open cost", "T-D integrity disagreement", "Trade Not Comparable". No assertion beyond the plan's wording except the extras listed above. Test fixture file `tests/support/review-fixture.ts` shared by all cases. |

| T4.15 | (case 1, rows 2 of the part sequence) | note | The two green rows with exit 1 for the frame part are a test fault, not production: the expected Stale price was written 39.00 and Market Data normalizes it to 39; the test was corrected and the next row is the genuine green. The green row with exit 1 before the Saved form was implemented is the red of the Saved assertion (relabelled red). Case 1 was grown part by part: summary and labels, frame with Stale context, evaluation, New form, Saved form, definition revision, save binding, expected Entry revision, each with its own red. | |
| T4.15 | should include the dated last-close and at-entry lines when the Review-date Mark is Missing, P13 | note | First-run pass, flagged for the user: two assertions of case 2 passed on the first run, the current figures Unavailable(MarkMissing) naming XYZ and the Review date, and the at-entry figures. Both were built by earlier tasks (T4.9 and T4.9-B2 in evaluate, composed by case 1's evaluation). Mutation 1: evaluate the current figures with the Stale Mark as if it were the Review-date Mark; command npx vitest run src/coordinators/daily-review/daily-review.test.ts -t "should include the dated last-close and at-entry lines when the Review-date Mark is Missing, P13"; first line AssertionError: expected { kind: 'Value', …(1) } to deeply equal { kind: 'Unavailable', …(2) }. Mutation 2: override atEntry with null; same command; first line AssertionError: expected null to deeply equal { label: 'AtEntry', …(4) }. Both restored; the file equals its pre-mutation copy. The dated last-close assertion and the no-exposure assertion had their own reds. | |
| T4.15 | should record nothing | note | First-run pass, flagged for the user: getTrade is read-only by construction, so the Before and After dump comparison passed on the first run (the red row above is the invalid red). Mutation: commit one put to the meta store inside getTrade's assembly; command npx vitest run src/coordinators/daily-review/daily-review.test.ts -t "should record nothing"; first line AssertionError: expected 'ChangedDuringAssembly' to be 'Ready' // Object.is equality (the write moves the Workspace revision during assembly, so the case fails before its dump comparison; the dump comparison itself was not isolated by the mutation). Restored; file equals its pre-mutation copy. | |
| T4.15 | should return ReviewChanged when the Trade's revision or its evidence no longer matches the overview binding | note | Three assertions, each with its own red: the Trade's FactRevision, the Review-date evidence snapshot (a Mark saved after the overview), and a Trade the Review no longer finds eligible (the red was the thrown placeholder error). | |
| T4.15 | should return the same attention reasons as the overview for an integrity disagreement and for facts that cannot be replayed | note | Added case beyond the four planned: getTrade returns the overview's attention item and, for unreplayable facts, no frame or evaluation (the frame lookup threw first, since the Instrument is not among the Review's requirements). The unreplayable assertions after the first had their own red (Market Data returned no frame for JKL). | |

| T4.21-B8 | should close an open form whose intention no longer fits the refreshed Mark and say the Mark changed and nothing was saved | note | | | Built in two parts, each with its own red. Red 1: Correct open on a key refreshed to Missing, form still open (red row above). Fix: close the form and show the notice for Correct only. The next run of the test is the red for part 2, Enter open on a key refreshed to Available (AssertionError: expected form to be null; the second row above marked green with exit=1 is that red, logged by the script before the generalization). Fix: a FITS table per form (Correct: Available; Enter: Missing or AcknowledgedUnavailable; Can't get a price: Missing). Notice assertions after the form check (alert text, no "values are kept", focus on the notice, Missing displayed, reopening clears the notice, no save call) had no red of their own: first-run passes, flagged. Mutations, each run with npx vitest run src/ui/screens/MarkEntry.test.tsx -t "should close an open form whose intention no longer fits": dropped setConflict closed -> TestingLibraryElementError: Unable to find an accessible element with the role "alert"; closed copy replaced by the kept copy -> AssertionError: expected ... not to contain 'values are kept'; focus effect limited to kept -> AssertionError: expected body to be the alert div (focus); Enter and Can't get a price made to fit Available -> AssertionError: expected form to be null. Each restored (git diff -- src showed the fix only). Not covered by any case: Can't get a price open on a key refreshed to Available (same FITS table, no assertion), and the Reopen-clears-notice and no-save-call assertions have no isolating mutation. B1 test in ReviewView.test.tsx edited: its refreshed key is now AcknowledgedUnavailable (Enter still fits) instead of Available, since Enter on Available is exactly what B8 closes. |

| T4.22 | | note | `npx vitest run src/ui/screens/ReviewTrade.test.tsx -t "<case>"` | | First-run passes, flagged. (1) Sub-assertions of the Trade-figures case other than the missing Review button had no red of their own (all production code for the case was absent at its red); mutation: at-entry line shows rewardFromEntry as Risk to stop -> AssertionError: expected '$200.00' to be '$60.00'. Restored. (2) Save Hold case: the walk rows following the returned completion had no own red; mutation: save handler does not copy post.completion -> AssertionError: expected [ 'Action missing', 'Action missing' ] to deeply equal [ 'Action saved', 'Action missing' ]. Restored. (3) Saved-Action case: submitting the binding with expectedEntryRevision passes the coordinator binding through unchanged (the first run after the display fix passed that part); mutation: binding sent with expectedEntryRevision 1 -> toHaveBeenCalledExactlyOnceWith failure. Restored. (4) ReviewChanged case: focus on the notice, refreshed binding used by getTrade; mutation: focus effect removed -> AssertionError: expected <button type="submit"> to be <div role="alert">; getTrade given review.binding -> AssertionError: expected last call to have been called with refreshed binding (after the test was strengthened so the first opens return the original binding: before that the mutation passed). Restored. (5) Intent case: trimmed Intent; mutation: raw intent sent -> toHaveBeenCalledExactlyOnceWith failure. Restored. (6) Leaving case: the later dirty assertions; mutation: dirty ignores Intent -> expected last call to have been called with [ true ]; unmount cleanup removed -> expected last call to have been called with [ false ]. Restored. |
| T4.22 | should start the next Trade form afresh | note | | | Code written ahead of red: `key={selected.tradeId}` on ReviewTrade was added with the ReviewChanged case (so Refresh would keep the form), before the case that needs it (a fresh form for the next Trade). Mutation proving the case now covers it: key removed -> AssertionError: expected radio Hold to have property checked with value true. Restored. Earlier green rows of ReviewChanged (exit 1) and the first green row of the Trade-figures case (exit 1) are failed green attempts fixed in the test (selector ambiguity, ambiguous figure lookup); the next green row of each is the passing one. |

Extra cases beyond the plan: no Save when not writable; Conflict, storage failure, rejection, refused write keep values; next Trade form afresh and the message when no Trade is next. Plan test names rephrased: apostrophe in "Trade's figures" left out. Assertions beyond plan: Web Storage not asserted (unavailable in the unit environment; e2e no-save covers it). |

The two red rows above are fixture errors (Error: coverage, from walking one session past the policy's first date), not valid reds; the test name's 1,260 became 1,255, the sessions inside session-policy coverage (2024-01-02 to 2028-12-29). After the fixture fix, the Open page counts passed at once, and the Review open's first assertion failed only because my guess of the gap request (getAllKeys) was wrong: the product issues one getAll on byInstrumentDate per Instrument (A47 counts requests, not their kind), so the assertion was corrected to the exact request list. Behavior built earlier by D2 T2.21 (Open page) and T4.5-T4.8 and T4.30-B1 (Market Data bounded reads, Review open). Mutation: the historical gap scan in `src/modules/market-data/market-data.ts` reads one date at a time instead of one range per Instrument. Command: `npx vitest run tests/integration/d4/query-count.test.ts -t "should issue the same bounded requests whether each Instrument has 100 or 1,255 sessions of history"`. First failure line: `AssertionError: expected [ { on: 'index', …(4) }, …(300) ] to deeply equal [ { on: 'index', …(4) }, …(6) ]`. Code restored (git diff -- src empty). | |

| T4.33 part 1 | clock | note | `npx playwright test e2e/d4` | | Finding, handled in `e2e/d4/support.ts` (`launchAt`), no product change: Chromium here has a native Temporal, which `temporal-polyfill` uses when present, and the production `systemClock` calls `Temporal.Now.instant()`; Playwright's clock pins `Date` only, so the first run saw real time (authoredTime 2026-10-29) in a case pinned to Monday 2026-10-12. `launchAt` pins `Date` with `context.clock.setFixedTime` and adds an init script that points `Temporal.Now.instant` at the pinned `Date.now()`. T4.31's `tools/bench-run.ts` pins only `Date` too, so its as-of pin may not reach the app in Chromium. |
| T4.33 part 1 | B4.7 | note | `npx playwright test e2e/d4/update.spec.ts` | | The d2 helper `watchForUpdatingMessage` kept its flag in the document; the update's Reload can load a second document and reset it, and the message shows only for a few milliseconds. d4 support adds `watchUpdatingAcrossDocuments`, which reports the sighting to the test through `context.exposeFunction`; the test polls it for up to 3 s (the binding call arrives a few milliseconds late). |

| T4.33-B1 | Review helpers wait for the save result | note | `npx playwright test e2e/d4` | exit 0; 7 passed (three runs in a row) | Bug task, test helper only (`e2e/d4/support.ts`). `saveAction` returned right after clicking Save; it now waits for the pressed form element to leave the page, which happens only when the save result renders (a Saved result replaces the form with the next Trade's form or none). `enterMark` and `acknowledgeMark` already wait for "Manual" and "Acknowledged Unavailable", which appear only after the result, so they are unchanged. | T4.31-B2 | Missing guard requires the full recorded set | note | scratch script calling `assertMissingRendered` (mutations: one recorded Missing symbol absent from the rows; one extra recorded symbol) | both throw; matching input passes | Bug task, harness only (`tools/bench/pin.ts`). The guard now compares the set of "Mark missing" symbols with the recorded set in both directions and keeps the zero-flagged failure. First failure line: `the Open list's Mark-missing symbols (AAA) are not the 2 Missing Instruments (AAA, CCC): not recorded as Missing: none; recorded as Missing but not flagged: CCC`. `npm run bench:expected` left `bench/expected-open-rows.json` unchanged; `npm run bench:run` exit 0. |
| T4.31-B3 | bench scripts close the browser when they fail | note | `npm run bench:expected` with `assertPinned(page, new Date(0))` forced | exit 1; no Chromium process on the temp profile | Bug task, tools only. The context is now closed in `finally` (a no-op when already closed) in `tools/bench-expected-rows.ts`, `tools/bench-run.ts` and `tools/bench-generate.ts`, so a failed wait or guard cannot leave Chromium on a removed profile. First failure line: `Error: the page is not pinned to the as-of instant 1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z`. Forced throw removed. |

MarkEntry keeps Cancel enabled. |
